> 144	/** Source-agnostic center-crop to `aspect`, then scale to (W,H). Mirrors segment-core's reframe so the zones
> 145	 * align with exactly the pixels the render will overlay. Commas inside min() are escaped for filtergraph. */
> 146	function reframeChain(aspect: number, w: number, h: number): string {
> 147	  const cw = `min(iw\\,ih*${aspect})`;
> 148	  const ch = `min(ih\\,iw/${aspect})`;
> 149	  return `crop=${cw}:${ch}:(iw-ow)/2:(ih-oh)/2,scale=${w}:${h}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop=${w}:${h},setsar=1`;
> 150	}
> 151	
> 152	interface RunResult { code: number; stderr: string; }
> 153	
> 154	function runFfmpeg(cmd: string, args: string[]): Promise<RunResult> {
> 155	  return new Promise((resolvePromise) => {
> 156	    let p;
> 157	    try {
> 158	      p = spawn(cmd, args, { stdio: ['ignore', 'ignore', 'pipe'] });
> 159	    } catch (e) {
> 160	      // spawn itself threw (binary missing) — fail-open, never surface to the caller.
> 161	      resolvePromise({ code: -1, stderr: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) });
> 162	      return;
> 163	    }
> 164	    let stderr = '';
> 165	    p.stderr.on('data', (d: Buffer) => { stderr += d.toString(); });
> 166	    p.on('error', (e: Error) => resolvePromise({ code: -1, stderr: e.message }));
> 167	    p.on('close', (code: number | null) => resolvePromise({ code: code ?? -1, stderr }));
> 168	  });
> 169	}

> 171	/** Order zones the render walks the grid in — kept fixed so per-zone metadata filenames are deterministic. */

> 173	
> 174	/** Build the single-frame, 6-zone FFmpeg pass for one instant `t`. Each zone writes its YAVG to its own file. */
> 175	function buildPassArgs(
> 176	  ffmpegPath: string, sourcePath: string, t: number, workW: number, workH: number, aspect: number,
> 177	  metaDir: string, sampleIdx: number,
> 178	): { args: string[]; files: Record<Zone, string> } {
> 179	  const reframe = reframeChain(aspect, workW, workH);
> 180	  const chains: string[] = [];
> 181	  const maps: string[] = [];
> 182	  const files = {} as Record<Zone, string>;
> 183	  ZONE_ORDER.forEach((zone, i) => {
> 184	    const r = ZONE_RECTS[zone];
> 185	    const file = join(metaDir, `s${sampleIdx}_${i}.txt`);
> 186	    files[zone] = file;
> 187	    // crop uses only fixed fractions of iw/ih (no commas, no caller data) → inert in the filtergraph.
> 188	    chains.push(
> 189	      `[s${i}]crop=iw*${r.w}:ih*${r.h}:iw*${r.x}:ih*${r.y},edgedetect,signalstats,metadata=print:file=${file}[o${i}]`,
> 190	    );
> 191	    maps.push('-map', `[o${i}]`, '-f', 'null', '-');
> 192	  });
> 193	  const splitLabels = ZONE_ORDER.map((_, i) => `[s${i}]`).join('');
> 194	  // select=eq(n\,0) → exactly the one seeked frame, so each null output emits a single frame then EOFs.
> 195	  const filterComplex =
> 196	    `[0:v]${reframe},select=eq(n\\,0),split=${ZONE_ORDER.length}${splitLabels};${chains.join(';')}`;
> 197	  const args = [
> 198	    '-v', 'error',
> 199	    '-ss', Math.max(0, t).toFixed(3),
> 200	    '-i', sourcePath,
> 201	    '-filter_complex', filterComplex,
> 202	    ...maps,
> 203	  ];
> 204	  return { args, files };
> 205	}
> 206	
> 207	/** Parse the MAX YAVG in a metadata=print file (single frame → one line, but MAX is robust to more). */
> 208	function readYavg(file: string): number | null {
> 209	  if (!existsSync(file)) return null;
> 210	  let text: string;
> 211	  try { text = readFileSync(file, 'utf8'); } catch { return null; }
> 212	  let max: number | null = null;
> 213	  const re = /YAVG=([-\d.]+)/g;
> 214	  let m: RegExpExecArray | null;
> 215	  while ((m = re.exec(text)) !== null) {
> 216	    const v = Number.parseFloat(m[1]);
> 217	    if (Number.isFinite(v)) max = max === null ? v : Math.max(max, v);
> 218	  }
> 219	  return max;
> 220	}
> 221	
> 222	/** Deterministic sample instants across the cut: start / mid / end (end nudged just inside the window). */
> 223	function sampleTimes(inSec: number, outSec: number, count: number): number[] {
> 224	  const start = Math.max(0, Number.isFinite(inSec) ? inSec : 0);
> 225	  const end = Math.max(start, Number.isFinite(outSec) ? outSec : start);
> 226	  const span = end - start;
> 227	  if (span <= 0.3 || count <= 1) return [start];
> 228	  const nudge = Math.min(0.1, span * 0.05);
> 229	  const mid = start + span / 2;
> 230	  const last = Math.max(start, end - nudge);
> 231	  const all = count >= 3 ? [start, mid, last] : [start, last];
> 232	  // Dedupe near-identical instants (a tiny window can collapse mid/last onto the endpoints).
> 233	  const out: number[] = [];
> 234	  for (const t of all) if (!out.some((u) => Math.abs(u - t) < 0.02)) out.push(t);
> 235	  return out;
> 236	}
> 237	
> 238	/** The fail-open default result — deterministic, unmeasured, never throws. */
> 239	function defaultPick(elapsedMs: number): ZonePick {
> 240	  const scores: ZoneScore[] = PREFERENCE.map((zone) => ({ zone, agitation: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, rect: ZONE_RECTS[zone] }));
> 241	  return { zone: DEFAULT_ZONE, rect: ZONE_RECTS[DEFAULT_ZONE], scores, degraded: true, elapsedMs };
> 242	}
> 243	
> 244	/**
> 245	 * Pick the calmest zone for the motion-graphics card over the cut window [in_sec, out_sec].
> 246	 *
> 247	 * Fail-open contract: NEVER throws. On any FFmpeg/source failure (or insufficient readings) it returns the
> 248	 * deterministic `lower-left` default with `degraded:true`. Zone choice is cosmetic — it must never break a render.
> 249	 */
> 250	export async function pickCalmestZone(
> 251	  sourcePath: string,
> 252	  inSec: number,
> 253	  outSec: number,
> 254	  opts: ZoneSaliencyOptions = {},
> 255	): Promise<ZonePick> {
> 256	  const t0 = performance.now();
> 257	  const workW = Math.max(2, Math.floor(opts.workW ?? 540));
> 258	  const workH = Math.max(2, Math.floor(opts.workH ?? 960));
> 259	  const aspect = opts.aspect && opts.aspect > 0 ? opts.aspect : 9 / 16;
> 260	  const sampleCount = Math.min(3, Math.max(1, Math.floor(opts.sampleCount ?? 3)));
> 261	  const ffmpegPath = opts.ffmpegPath ?? 'ffmpeg';
> 262	
> 263	  if (!existsSync(sourcePath)) return defaultPick(performance.now() - t0);
> 264	
> 265	  const times = sampleTimes(inSec, outSec, sampleCount);
> 266	  // Per-zone temporal MAX of agitation — the worst instant governs so the card never lands where the footage
> 267	  // is briefly busy. Undefined until a zone gets at least one reading.
> 268	  const maxAgitation = {} as Partial<Record<Zone, number>>;
> 269	  let metaDir: string | null = null;
> 270	  try {
> 271	    metaDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'zone-saliency-'));
> 272	    for (let s = 0; s < times.length; s++) {
> 273	      const { args, files } = buildPassArgs(ffmpegPath, sourcePath, times[s], workW, workH, aspect, metaDir, s);
> 274	      const res = await runFfmpeg(ffmpegPath, args);
> 275	      if (res.code !== 0) continue; // fail-soft per instant; other instants may still succeed
> 276	      for (const zone of ZONE_ORDER) {
> 277	        const v = readYavg(files[zone]);
> 278	        if (v === null) continue;
> 279	        const prev = maxAgitation[zone];
> 280	        maxAgitation[zone] = prev === undefined ? v : Math.max(prev, v);
> 281	      }
> 282	    }
> 283	  } catch {
> 284	    return defaultPick(performance.now() - t0);
> 285	  } finally {
> 286	    if (metaDir) rmSync(metaDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
> 287	  }
> 288	
> 289	  // Only zones that (a) got a reading and (b) fit the card minimum are selectable.
> 290	  const selectable = ZONE_ORDER.filter((z) => maxAgitation[z] !== undefined && zoneFits(z));
> 291	  if (selectable.length === 0) return defaultPick(performance.now() - t0);
> 292	
> 293	  const scores: ZoneScore[] = selectable
> 294	    .map((zone) => ({ zone, agitation: maxAgitation[zone] as number, rect: ZONE_RECTS[zone] }))
> 295	    // Calmest first: bucket agitation by CALM_EPS so near-equal calm zones defer to PREFERENCE (transitive).
> 296	    .sort((a, b) => {
> 297	      const ba = Math.round(a.agitation / CALM_EPS);
> 298	      const bb = Math.round(b.agitation / CALM_EPS);
> 299	      if (ba !== bb) return ba - bb;
> 300	      return PREF_INDEX[a.zone] - PREF_INDEX[b.zone];
> 301	    });
> 302	
> 303	  const chosen = scores[0].zone;
> 304	  return {
> 305	    zone: chosen,
> 306	    rect: ZONE_RECTS[chosen],
> 307	    scores,
> 308	    degraded: false,
> 309	    elapsedMs: performance.now() - t0,
> 310	  };
> 311	}
> 312	
> ```

> 100	/** Source-agnostic center-crop to `ar`, then scale to (W,H). Keeps the largest centered rect of the target aspect. */
> 101	function reframeChain(ar: number, W: number, H: number): string {
> 102	  // crop w = min(iw, ih*ar); h = min(ih, iw/ar); centered. Expressions are evaluated by FFmpeg per input dims.

> 204	
> 205	/** Segment a master into N clips per the cut-spec. Serial (deterministic, memory-safe for a long master). */
> 206	export async function segmentVideo(spec: SegmentSpec): Promise<ClipResult[]> {
> 207	  if (!existsSync(spec.sourcePath)) throw new Error(`source_not_found: ${spec.sourcePath}`);
> 208	  if (!Array.isArray(spec.clips) || spec.clips.length === 0) throw new Error('cut_spec_empty');
> 209	  mkdirSync(spec.outDir, { recursive: true });
> 210	  const out: ClipResult[] = [];
> 211	  for (let i = 0; i < spec.clips.length; i++) {
> 212	    const c = spec.clips[i];
> 213	    if (!(c.out_sec > c.in_sec) || c.in_sec < 0) throw new Error(`clip_${i}_invalid_range`);
> 214	    out.push(await renderClip(spec, c, i));
> 215	  }
> 216	  return out;
> 217	}

Prova ocular deste ciclo: sujeito (barras `testsrc2`) à direita → `pick.zone=lower-left`; espelhado → `lower-right`; cartão pousou no vazio nas duas. |
> 35	| A2 | **O PIOR instante governa (temporal MAX).** A footage fica ocupada de forma intermitente; amostrar só um frame elegeria uma zona que fica calma **por um átimo**. | `pickCalmestZone` amostra `start / mid / end` (`sampleTimes`, até 3, clamp 1..3) e toma o **MAX** do `YAVG` por zona (`maxAgitation`). Um cartão nunca pousa onde a footage é brevemente calma mas depois enche. |
> 36	| A3 | **Coluna central é estruturalmente excluída.** O sujeito vive quase sempre na faixa central 9:16. | A grade é **2 colunas** com um **CENTER GUTTER largo `[0.42,0.58]`** (`COL_LEFT_X=0.045`, `COL_RIGHT_X=0.58`) — a "banda vertical". `center-stack` existe no template como superset, mas **NÃO é elegível por saliência** (§4.1 da Amendment 12). |
> 37	| A4 | **Empate por float é ruído sub-perceptual.** Duas zonas com `YAVG` quase idêntico não têm diferença visível de calma. | `CALM_EPS=0.5` **bucketiza** o score; zonas na mesma faixa deferem à `PREFERENCE` (transitivo). Sem isso, o motor "caçaria" flutuação de decoder e a zona pularia entre renders equivalentes. |
> 38	| A5 | **A saliência é COSMÉTICA — nunca pode derrubar um render.** Um cartão num canto um pouco mais ocupado é uma falha muito mais barata que um re-encode multi-minuto abortado. | Contrato **fail-open** (`zone-saliency.ts:246` — "NEVER throws"): fonte ausente / FFmpeg ausente / zona sem leitura → `defaultPick` = `lower-left`, `degraded:true`. O `segment-core` embrulha a chamada em `try/catch` **e** loga o `degraded` — falha silenciosa é a classe de falso-sucesso que este projeto já levou (`segment-core.ts:133-146`). |
> 39	| A6 | **Viés de banda deliberado.** A ordem de desempate não é arbitrária. | `PREFERENCE = [lower-*, mid-*, upper-*]` (banda de baixo primeiro): é o terço convencional de legenda e o **menos** provável de cobrir um rosto (que costuma sentar no terço superior/central). `DEFAULT_ZONE = lower-left` é também o fail-open. |
> 40	
> 41	## Sequence — os passos de `pickCalmestZone` (cada um com critério material)
> 42	
> 43	| # | Passo | Executor | Sucesso material |
> 44	|---|-------|----------|------------------|
> 45	| 0 | O `segment-core` só chama a sonda quando `resolveCaptionStyle(clip.caption_style) === MOTION_GRAPHICS_STYLE`. Os outros estilos mantêm os `beats` sem `zone` e **não** pagam a sonda. | `segment-core.ts:128` | estilo karaokê → nenhum `spawn` de saliência (0 custo). |
> 46	| 1 | **Existência da fonte:** `existsSync(sourcePath)` falso → `defaultPick` imediato (fail-open A5). | `pickCalmestZone` | caminho existe em disco, senão `degraded:true` sem `spawn`. |
> 47	| 2 | **Instantes de amostra:** `sampleTimes(inSec, outSec, count)` → `start / mid / end` (fim empurrado `nudge` para dentro da janela), dedupe de instantes < 0,02 s (janela minúscula colapsa mid/end nas pontas). | `sampleTimes` | 1..3 instantes finitos dentro de `[inSec, outSec]`. |
> 48	| 3 | **Grade de 6 zonas candidatas:** 2 colunas × 3 linhas dentro da safe-area (laterais ~4,5%, topo ~7%, base ~13%), center gutter `[0.42,0.58]`. Cada célula 0,375×0,24 do canvas (405×461 px @ 1080×1920 — acima do `MIN_ZONE_*`, logo o cartão de 2 níveis sempre cabe). | `ZONE_RECTS` | 6 rects fixos; nenhuma na coluna central. |
> 49	| 4 | **Passe FFmpeg por instante:** `[0:v] reframe 9:16 → select=eq(n\,0) → split=6 → (por zona) crop→edgedetect→signalstats→metadata=print:file=<zona>`. Reframe **espelha** o `reframeChain` do `segment-core` (as zonas alinham com os pixels que o render vai sobrepor). | `buildPassArgs` + `runFfmpeg` | `exit 0`; um arquivo `YAVG` por zona escrito no `metaDir` (tmp). Fail-soft por instante: `exit≠0` → pula o instante, outros ainda contam. |
> 50	| 5 | **Leitura + agregação temporal:** `readYavg` parseia o `YAVG` (MAX-robusto) de cada arquivo; `maxAgitation[zone] = max(anteriores, atual)` — o **PIOR instante governa** (A2). | `readYavg` | cada zona lida ganha um `YAVG` finito ≥ 0. |
> 51	| 6 | **Filtro de elegibilidade:** só zonas que (a) tiveram leitura **e** (b) cabem o cartão (`zoneFits` ≥ `MIN_ZONE_W_FRAC`/`_H_FRAC`) entram. Nenhuma elegível → `defaultPick` (A5). | `pickCalmestZone` | `selectable.length > 0`, senão `degraded:true`. |
> 52	| 7 | **Escolha da mais calma + viés de banda:** ordena por `round(YAVG/CALM_EPS)` asc; empate na mesma faixa → `PREF_INDEX` (banda de baixo primeiro, A6). `scores[0]` é a escolhida. | `pickCalmestZone` | `zone`/`rect`/`scores[]` ranqueados, `degraded:false`, `elapsedMs` medido. |
> 53	| 8 | **Injeção no beat:** o `segment-core` carimba `zone` em **cada** beat (`beatProps = beats.map(b => ({...b, zone}))`) + carrega o `zone` de topo. Uma zona calma governa a janela inteira do clipe (Fatia 1 — zoneamento por-beat/face-aware é a Fatia 2). O template lê `b.zone` e coerça zona inválida para `'lower-left'` (allowlist fechada no template). | `segment-core.ts:150` | props do render carregam a zona eleita; `renderAlphaFrames` produz o PNG RGBA no canto certo. |
> 54	
> 55	## Verification gates
> 56	
> 57	Anticorpo re-executável: **`scripts/qa/smoke-zone-saliency.ts`** (zero-custo, hermético, offline — sintetiza fixtures com `ffmpeg lavfi`: uma metade `color=gray` calma + uma metade `testsrc` ocupada, resposta correta conhecida a priori; **nunca** toca um master real, a rede ou o DB). **Rodar antes de mexer na grade de zonas, na métrica de agitação ou nas partições de amostra.**
> 58	
> 59	| Gate | O que prova |
> 60	|------|-------------|
> 61	| **G1 — sujeito à DIREITA → zona à ESQUERDA.** | Detalhe (`testsrc`) na metade direita sobre metade esquerda lisa ⇒ `!degraded && centerX(rect) < 0.5 && scores.length === 6`. A métrica de agitação de fato encontra o lado calmo. (Prova ocular deste ciclo: `pick.zone=lower-left`, cartão "O PONTO É [FOCO]" pousou sobre o vazio à esquerda.) |
> 62	| **G2 — sujeito à ESQUERDA → zona à DIREITA.** | O espelho de G1: `centerX(rect) > 0.5`. Prova que a escolha **segue o sujeito**, não um viés fixo de lado. (Ocular: `pick.zone=lower-right`, cartão à direita, `FOCO` sobre o vazio.) |
> 63	| **G3 — fail-open em fonte inexistente.** | Caminho para arquivo inexistente ⇒ `degraded===true && zone===DEFAULT_ZONE` (`lower-left`) **e NÃO lança**. Prova o contrato A5: a escolha cosmética nunca derruba um render. |
> 64	| **G4 — custo por corte medido (< ~2 s).** | Lê o `elapsedMs` **real** dos picks de G1/G2 (Lei 1 — o custo verdadeiro, não estimado) e afirma `< 2000 ms`. Prova que a sonda fica dentro do orçamento 0,3–0,9 s/corte que justifica rodá-la síncrona antes de cada render. |
> 65	
> 66	**Custo material:** rail grátis preservado — `charged_mco=0`, US$ 0. Nenhuma migration, nenhum `/security-review` de banco (`zone` é campo dentro do jsonb `video_renders.composition` já existente — migration `20260624120000`); o `/security-review` incide sobre o **motor** (spawn com array, filtergraph sem dado do caller).
> 67	
> 68	## Recovery path
> 69	
> 70	| Falha | Diagnóstico | Ação exata |
> 71	|-------|-------------|------------|
> 72	| `degraded:true` num corte (log `[repurpose] zone-saliency degraded for clip N — probe did not measure, using default zone 'lower-left'`). | FFmpeg/`edgedetect` ausente no host, master ilegível na janela, ou nenhuma zona rendeu leitura. **O render prosseguiu** (fail-open) — a única perda é a inteligência de posição. | `which ffmpeg` + `ffmpeg -filters | grep edgedetect` no host do worker. Rodar `smoke-zone-saliency.ts` — se ele passar, o host está são e o problema é a fonte daquele corte (checar a janela do clipe com o decode-probe: `docs/processes/video-repurpose-source-probe.md`). **Nunca** tratar `degraded` como erro de render — é telemetria de qualidade, não caminho de falha. |
> 73	| `pickCalmestZone threw` (log `[repurpose] zone-saliency threw for clip N (kept default 'lower-left')`). | Falha **inesperada** — o contrato é no-throw; chegar aqui é bug do motor, não da fonte. O `try/catch` do `segment-core` manteve o `DEFAULT_ZONE`. | Reproduzir com o master + janela reais num driver de scratchpad (molde: importar `pickCalmestZone` e olhar `pick`); corrigir o motor; re-rodar o smoke. O render **não** precisa esperar o fix (fail-open já cobriu). |
> 74	| Cartão pousou **sobre o sujeito** apesar de `degraded:false`. | **Limite conhecido, não bug** (ver abaixo): a zona estatisticamente mais calma coincidiu com o rosto porque o fundo atrás do rosto era liso/claro (baixa densidade de borda). A saliência mede calma, não rosto. | Aceite Fatia 1: re-autorar o cut-spec com `zone` explícita (superset), **ou** trocar o `caption_style` do corte, **ou** escalar para o gatilho da Fatia 2 (OTD-VR-016). Registrar o corte como evidência para o gatilho volume/qualidade. |
> 75	
> 76	## Success signal
> 77	
> 78	**O texto pousa no vazio — provado OCULARMENTE por render** (Lei 1 para mídia; "renderizou sem erro" não é prova). Neste ciclo, dois renders compostos (cartão alpha `motion-graphics-hero-9x16` sobre footage 9:16 com sujeito num lado):
> 79	
> 80	- **Sujeito à DIREITA** → `pick.zone=lower-left`, `degraded=false`, `elapsedMs=923` → cartão "O PONTO É [FOCO]" (kicker branco + hero ciano `#22D3EE` + colchetes arquiteturais) pousou **inteiro sobre a metade escura calma à esquerda**, sem colidir com as barras de cor à direita.
> 81	- **Sujeito à ESQUERDA** → `pick.zone=lower-right`, `degraded=false` → cartão espelhado (alinhado à direita) com `FOCO` sobre **o vazio escuro à direita**, o sujeito à esquerda intacto.
> 82	
> 83	O sinal de que o SOP está vivo é duplo: (a) o smoke `scripts/qa/smoke-zone-saliency.ts` sai **G1..G4 verdes** com o `elapsedMs` real reportado; e (b) — o que a estatística **não** garante — o **olho** confirma, por render, que o cartão não cobre o sujeito. Se algum dia a métrica passar mas o olho reprovar, o olho vence (é a Fatia 2 batendo à porta).
> 84	
> 85	## Notas de design
> 86	
> 87	- **Por que Opção B (saliência US$ 0) e não Opção C (MediaPipe face-aware) agora:** shipar o zero-custo primeiro (Padrão 20 Prioritization) e só pagar a complexidade do face-detection quando a aproximação da Fatia 1 **provar-se insuficiente** por evidência (não por suposição). A Opção C está registrada como OTD-VR-016, não descartada.
> 88	- **Uma zona por clipe, não por beat:** o cartão **não** pula de canto entre beats — pular seria ruído visual. A janela inteira do corte governa por uma zona (Fatia 1). Re-eleger por janela de beat é refinamento possível, fora do escopo.
> 89	- **Reframe do motor ESPELHA o do `segment-core`** (`reframeChain` idêntico) — sem isso, as zonas pontuariam pixels que o render não sobrepõe, e a escolha seria contra o quadro errado.
> 90	- **`stderr`/`exit` por instante é fail-soft, não fail-hard:** um instante que falha é pulado; os outros ainda votam. Só a ausência **total** de leitura cai no default. Diferente do decode-probe (`source-probe.md`), onde `stderr` não-vazio é veredito de **falha** — lá a integridade é load-bearing; aqui a posição é cosmética.
> 91	
> 92	## Limite conhecido (Lei 1 — honestidade material)
> 93	
> 94	**Saliência mede CALMA, não ROSTO.** `edgedetect→YAVG` é um *proxy* de espaço negativo por densidade de borda. Um fundo liso e claro atrás de um rosto lê como **"calmo"** mesmo com o rosto ali; um cartão pode aterrissar sobre um rosto se essa for a zona estatisticamente mais calma. A Fatia 1 aceita conscientemente essa aproximação zero-custo. A **prova de fuga-de-rosto** — bounding box real do rosto → cartão na metade oposta — é a **Fatia 2 (OTD-VR-016)**: MediaPipe FaceDetection WASM rodando **dentro do Chromium** do render, self-hosted US$ 0 (NÃO Vision MCP, NÃO Adobe pago), **DEFERIDA e gated**. Ela acopla ao rail frames-in-browser (OTD-VR-014 Saída b) — é mudança de rail, não ajuste de motor — e só volta à mesa quando Vision QA (ou métrica de criativo) constatar que a zona calma da Fatia 1 aterrissa texto sobre rostos numa taxa que fere a qualidade percebida, OU quando o volume tornar a inspeção ocular por corte inviável. Enquanto DEFERIDA, `pickCalmestZone` **não** injeta frames no browser, **não** roda MediaPipe e **não** faz composição dividida — ele posiciona por zona calma e ponto.
> 95	
> 96	## Rastreabilidade
> 97	
> 98	- **BoK:** BR-VR-006 (shorts motion-graphic) · FR-VR-023 (zona por saliência, Opção B US$ 0) · Gramática §4.1/§4.3 · Amendment 12 §7 (Pattern Conformance) — `docs/bok/video-repurpose/12-amendment-motion-graphics.md`.
> 99	- **Padrões agênticos implementados:** #5 Tool Use (reusa `signalstats`/`edgedetect` FFmpeg) · #11 Goal Setting & Monitoring (objetivo "o cartão pousa na zona mais calma" + smoke que mede) · #12 Exception Handling (fail-open `lower-left` + telemetria `degraded`) · #16 Resource-Aware (Opção B US$ 0 sobre a Opção C paga) · #20 Prioritization (Fatia 1 antes da Fatia 2) — `docs/architecture/agentic-vision.md`.
> 100	- **OTD aberta:** **OTD-VR-016** (Fatia 2 — composição dividida consciente do rosto via MediaPipe WASM in-Chromium; DEFERIDA, gated).
> 101	- **OTDs relacionadas:** OTD-VR-014 (frames-in-browser — o veículo técnico da Fatia 2) · OTD-VR-015 (legibilidade adaptativa ao fundo).
> 102	- **Sem migration:** `zone` é campo dentro do jsonb existente `video_renders.composition` (migration `20260624120000`) — nenhuma alteração de schema disparada por esta feature.
> 103	
> ```

> 117	function runFfmpeg(args: string[]): Promise<void> {
> 118	  return new Promise((res, rej) => {
> 119	    const p = spawn('ffmpeg', args, { stdio: ['ignore', 'ignore', 'pipe'] });
> 120	    let err = '';
> 121	    p.stderr.on('data', (d) => { err += d.toString(); });
> 122	    p.on('error', rej);
> 123	    p.on('close', (code) => (code === 0 ? res() : rej(new Error(`ffmpeg exit ${code}: ${err.slice(-800)}`))));
> 124	  });
> 125	}

> 148	// ZERO paid API (the composition/animation layer stays 100% free). Each image is held for `seconds_per_image`,
> 149	// scaled + letterbox-padded to the canvas (no crop → the generated frame is never mutilated), concatenated to
> 150	// H.264 yuv420p, deterministic (fixed fps + bitexact flags, mirrors encodePngFramesBitexact NFR-VS-016).
> 151	export interface ImageSequenceSpec {
> 152	  images: string[];            // ordered absolute paths to pre-generated frames (PNG/JPG)
> 153	  seconds_per_image?: number;  // hold per frame in seconds (default 2.5)
> 154	  fps?: number;                // output fps (default 30)
> 155	  width?: number;              // canvas width (default 1080)
> 156	  height?: number;             // canvas height (default 1920 → 9:16)
> 157	}
> 158	
> 159	export async function encodeImageSequence(spec: ImageSequenceSpec, outPath: string): Promise<RenderResult> {
> 160	  const started = Date.now();
> 161	  const images = (spec.images ?? []).map((p) => resolve(p));
> 162	  if (images.length === 0) throw new Error('encodeImageSequence: no images provided');
> 163	  for (const p of images) if (!existsSync(p)) throw new Error(`encodeImageSequence: missing frame ${p}`);
> 164	  const fps = spec.fps ?? 30;
> 165	  const secPer = spec.seconds_per_image ?? 2.5;
> 166	  const W = spec.width ?? 1080;
> 167	  const H = spec.height ?? 1920;
> 168	
> 169	  // FFmpeg concat demuxer: hold each image for `secPer`s. The demuxer drops the final entry's duration,
> 170	  // so the last file is repeated once (documented ffmpeg quirk) to honor its hold time.
> 171	  const listDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'hf-seq-'));
> 172	  const esc = (p: string) => p.replace(/'/g, "'\\''");
> 173	  const lines: string[] = [];
> 174	  for (const p of images) { lines.push(`file '${esc(p)}'`); lines.push(`duration ${secPer}`); }
> 175	  lines.push(`file '${esc(images[images.length - 1])}'`);
> 176	  const listPath = join(listDir, 'frames.txt');
> 177	  writeFileSync(listPath, lines.join('\n') + '\n');
> 178	
> 179	  try {
> 180	    await runFfmpeg([
> 181	      '-y',
> 182	      '-f', 'concat', '-safe', '0', '-i', listPath,
> 183	      // scale to fit, pad to the exact 9:16 canvas (letterbox, no crop), normalize SAR, fix fps
> 184	      '-vf', `scale=${W}:${H}:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,pad=${W}:${H}:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2:color=black,setsar=1,fps=${fps}`,
> 185	      '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'medium', '-crf', '20',
> 186	      '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p', '-r', String(fps),
> 187	      '-movflags', '+faststart', '-fflags', '+bitexact', '-flags:v', '+bitexact', '-map_metadata', '-1',
> 188	      outPath,
> 189	    ]);
> 190	  } finally {
> 191	    rmSync(listDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
> 192	  }
> 193	  if (!existsSync(outPath) || statSync(outPath).size < 1024) throw new Error('encodeImageSequence: output missing or too small');
> 194	  const probed = await ffprobeVideo(outPath);
> 195	  return {
> 196	    outPath,
> 197	    bytes: statSync(outPath).size,
> 198	    width: probed.width,
> 199	    height: probed.height,
> 200	    frames: probed.frames,
> 201	    fps: probed.fps,
> 202	    durationMs: Date.now() - started,
> 203	  };
> 204	}
> 205	
> 206	// ── Free image-to-video (Ken Burns) — the Sovereign's "animar as imagens geradas 5-8s cada" ──────────────────
> 207	// Deterministic pan/zoom over a single still → an animated 9:16 clip via FFmpeg zoompan. ZERO paid API — the free
> 208	// substitute for neural i2v (Higgsfield DoP/Kling/Seedance), which stay as the paid opt-in. Upscale 2× first so
> 209	// zoompan's integer stepping stays smooth (documented anti-jitter trick).
> 210	export interface KenBurnsSpec {
> 211	  image: string;               // absolute path to the still to animate
> 212	  duration_s?: number;         // default 6 (the 5-8s range)
> 213	  fps?: number;                // default 30
> 214	  width?: number;              // 1080
> 215	  height?: number;             // 1920 → 9:16
> 216	  zoom?: 'in' | 'out';         // default 'in'
> 217	  max_zoom?: number;           // default 1.25
> 218	}
> 219	
> 220	export async function encodeKenBurns(spec: KenBurnsSpec, outPath: string): Promise<RenderResult> {
> 221	  const started = Date.now();
> 222	  const img = resolve(spec.image);
> 223	  if (!existsSync(img)) throw new Error(`encodeKenBurns: missing image ${img}`);
> 224	  const fps = spec.fps ?? 30;
> 225	  const dur = spec.duration_s ?? 6;
> 226	  const W = spec.width ?? 1080;
> 227	  const H = spec.height ?? 1920;
> 228	  const totalFrames = Math.round(dur * fps);
> 229	  const maxZoom = spec.max_zoom ?? 1.25;
> 230	  const step = (maxZoom - 1) / totalFrames;
> 231	  // Ramp 1 → maxZoom (in) or maxZoom → 1 (out) across the clip; `on` = output frame index (deterministic).
> 232	  const zExpr = (spec.zoom ?? 'in') === 'in'
> 233	    ? `min(zoom+${step.toFixed(6)},${maxZoom})`
> 234	    : `max(${maxZoom.toFixed(6)}-on*${step.toFixed(6)},1.0)`;
> 235	  const vf =
> 236	    `scale=${W * 2}:${H * 2},` +
> 237	    `zoompan=z='${zExpr}':d=${totalFrames}:x='iw/2-(iw/zoom/2)':y='ih/2-(ih/zoom/2)':s=${W}x${H}:fps=${fps},` +
> 238	    `setsar=1`;
> 239	  await runFfmpeg([
> 240	    '-y',
> 241	    '-loop', '1', '-i', img,
> 242	    '-t', String(dur),
> 243	    '-vf', vf,
> 244	    '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'medium', '-crf', '20',
> 245	    '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p', '-r', String(fps),
> 246	    '-movflags', '+faststart', '-map_metadata', '-1',
> 247	    outPath,
> 248	  ]);
> 249	  if (!existsSync(outPath) || statSync(outPath).size < 1024) throw new Error('encodeKenBurns: output missing or too small');
> 250	  const probed = await ffprobeVideo(outPath);
> 251	  return {
> 252	    outPath,
> 253	    bytes: statSync(outPath).size,
> 254	    width: probed.width,
> 255	    height: probed.height,
> 256	    frames: probed.frames,
> 257	    fps: probed.fps,
> 258	    durationMs: Date.now() - started,
> 259	  };
> 260	}
> 261	
> 262	// ── Studio-project render (FR-VS-047 → FR-VS-050 native, SDD §VS-UI-C) ───────────────────────────────────────
> 263	// Default engine = NATIVE on @hyperframes/core (Apache-2.0, LICENSE shipped in the npm tarball — attribution:
> 264	// © HeyGen, https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes). The CLI wrap is kept as emergency rollback only
> 265	// (STUDIO_RENDER_ENGINE=cli) — its npm artifact ships no license grant (OTD-VS-020).
> 266	// Single-tenant Usuário Zero root (OTD-VS-019 multi-tenant deferred). Overridable by env for portability/tests.
> 267	const STUDIO_PROJECTS_ROOT = process.env.STUDIO_PROJECTS_ROOT ?? '/home/ubuntu/.mcorch/video-studio/projects';
> 268	const HF_CLI = process.env.HYPERFRAMES_CLI ?? '/home/ubuntu/.bun/install/global/node_modules/hyperframes/dist/cli.js';
> 269	const HF_NODE = process.env.HYPERFRAMES_NODE ?? '/home/ubuntu/.local/bin/node'; // node v22 (hyperframes needs >=22)
> 270	// Preset → output dimensions (mirrors the reference CLI's CANVAS_DIMENSIONS).
> 271	const RESOLUTION_DIMENSIONS: Record<string, { width: number; height: number }> = {
> 272	  portrait: { width: 1080, height: 1920 },
> 273	  landscape: { width: 1920, height: 1080 },
> 274	  square: { width: 1080, height: 1080 },
> 275	  'portrait-4k': { width: 2160, height: 3840 },
> 276	  'landscape-4k': { width: 3840, height: 2160 },
> 277	  'square-4k': { width: 2160, height: 2160 },
> 278	};
> 279	const VALID_RESOLUTIONS = new Set(Object.keys(RESOLUTION_DIMENSIONS));
> 280	
> 281	/** ffprobe a video → real dimensions/frames/fps (the renderer derives them from the composition + preset). */
> 282	function ffprobeVideo(path: string): Promise<{ width: number; height: number; frames: number; fps: number }> {
> 283	  return new Promise((res, rej) => {
> 284	    const p = spawn('ffprobe', ['-v', 'error', '-select_streams', 'v:0',
> 285	      '-show_entries', 'stream=width,height,nb_frames,r_frame_rate,duration', '-of', 'json', path],
> 286	      { stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'ignore'] });
> 287	    let out = '';
> 288	    p.stdout.on('data', (d) => { out += d.toString(); });
> 289	    p.on('error', rej);
> 290	    p.on('close', (code) => {
> 291	      if (code !== 0) return rej(new Error(`ffprobe exit ${code}`));
> 292	      try {
> 293	        const s = (JSON.parse(out).streams ?? [])[0] ?? {};
> 294	        const [rn, rd] = String(s.r_frame_rate ?? '30/1').split('/').map(Number);
> 295	        const fps = rd ? rn / rd : (rn || 30);
> 296	        const frames = Number(s.nb_frames) || Math.round((Number(s.duration) || 0) * fps);
> 297	        res({ width: Number(s.width) || 0, height: Number(s.height) || 0, frames, fps });
> 298	      } catch (e) { rej(e); }
> 299	    });
> 300	  });
> 301	}
> 302	
> 303	/**
> 304	 * Render a composition spec to a deterministic 9:16 MP4 at `outPath`.
> 305	 * Throws on any failure (the worker translates that to finalize_video_render(failed, refund)).
> 306	 */
> 307	export async function renderComposition(spec: RenderSpec, outPath: string): Promise<RenderResult> {
> 308	  const t0 = Date.now();
> 309	  const template = spec.template && VALID_TEMPLATES.has(spec.template) ? spec.template : 'viral-short-9x16';
> 310	  const fps = Math.max(1, Math.min(60, spec.fps ?? 30));
> 311	  const durationS = Math.max(1, Math.min(60, spec.duration_s ?? 6));
> 312	  const width = spec.width ?? 1080;
> 313	  const height = spec.height ?? 1920;
> 314	  const totalFrames = Math.round(durationS * fps);
> 315	
> 316	  const templatePath = resolve(__dirname, 'templates', `${template}.html`);
> 317	  if (!existsSync(templatePath)) throw new Error(`template_not_found: ${template}`);
> 318	
> 319	  const frameDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'hf-frames-'));
> 320	  const browser = await chromium.launch({
> 321	    headless: true,
> 322	    // Single-tenant Usuário Zero (OTD-VA-011). Multi-tenant hardening (seccomp/egress-only container) = User 1.
> 323	    args: ['--no-sandbox', '--disable-dev-shm-usage', '--force-color-profile=srgb', '--disable-lcd-text'],
> 324	  });
> 325	  try {
> 326	    const page = await browser.newPage({
> 327	      viewport: { width, height },
> 328	      deviceScaleFactor: 1,
> 329	    });
> 330	    await page.goto(`file://${templatePath}`, { waitUntil: 'load' });
> 331	    await page.evaluate((props) => (window as HfWindow).applyProps(props), spec.props as Record<string, unknown>);
> 332	
> 333	    // Deterministically wait for the product image (if any) to settle — bounded, no wallclock leakage into frames.
> 334	    await page.evaluate(() => new Promise<void>((res) => {
> 335	      const img = document.getElementById('mediaImg') as HTMLImageElement | null;
> 336	      if (!img || img.style.display === 'none' || img.complete) return res();
> 337	      const done = () => res();
> 338	      img.addEventListener('load', done, { once: true });
> 339	      img.addEventListener('error', done, { once: true });
> 340	      setTimeout(done, 8000);
> 341	    }));
> 342	
> 343	    // Frame-by-frame capture (driven by index, not time → reproducible).
> 344	    for (let i = 0; i < totalFrames; i++) {
> 345	      await page.evaluate(([idx, total]) => (window as HfWindow).applyFrame(idx, total), [i, totalFrames] as [number, number]);
> 346	      await page.screenshot({
> 347	        path: join(frameDir, `f_${String(i).padStart(5, '0')}.png`),
> 348	        clip: { x: 0, y: 0, width, height },
> 349	        animations: 'disabled',
> 350	      });
> 351	    }
> 352	    await browser.close();
> 353	
> 354	    await encodePngFramesBitexact(frameDir, fps, outPath);
> 355	
> 356	    if (!existsSync(outPath)) throw new Error('render_produced_no_file');
> 357	    const bytes = statSync(outPath).size;
> 358	    if (bytes < 100 * 1024) throw new Error(`render_too_small: ${bytes} bytes (< 100KB, Lei 1 gate)`);
> 359	
> 360	    return { outPath, bytes, width, height, frames: totalFrames, fps, durationMs: Date.now() - t0 };
> 361	  } finally {
> 362	    await browser.close().catch(() => {});
> 363	    rmSync(frameDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
> 364	  }
> 365	}
> 366	
> 367	export interface AlphaFramesResult {
> 368	  frameDir: string;
> 369	  framePattern: string;   // e.g. 'f_%05d.png'
> 370	  frames: number;
> 371	  fps: number;
> 372	  width: number;
> 373	  height: number;
> 374	}
> 375	
> 376	/**
> 377	 * Render a composition's TEXT/GRAPHIC layer to a TRANSPARENT PNG sequence (RGBA, no background) for compositing
> 378	 * OVER real footage (video-repurpose FR-VR-011/012). Same deterministic index-driven loop as renderComposition,
> 379	 * but page.screenshot({ omitBackground: true }) → alpha preserved, and NO MP4 encode (yuv420p would drop alpha).
> 380	 * The template MUST have a transparent html/body background (e.g. 'viral-caption-overlay-9x16').
> 381	 * Injects fps + durationSec into props so time-based templates map frame index → clip seconds.
> 382	 * Returns the frame dir + %05d pattern; the CALLER overlays with FFmpeg and cleans the dir up.
> 383	 */
> 384	export async function renderAlphaFrames(spec: RenderSpec, outDir?: string): Promise<AlphaFramesResult> {
> 385	  const template = spec.template && VALID_TEMPLATES.has(spec.template) ? spec.template : 'viral-caption-overlay-9x16';
> 386	  const fps = Math.max(1, Math.min(60, spec.fps ?? 30));
> 387	  const durationS = Math.max(1, Math.min(60, spec.duration_s ?? 6));
> 388	  const width = spec.width ?? 1080;
> 389	  const height = spec.height ?? 1920;
> 390	  const totalFrames = Math.round(durationS * fps);
> 391	
> 392	  const templatePath = resolve(__dirname, 'templates', `${template}.html`);
> 393	  if (!existsSync(templatePath)) throw new Error(`template_not_found: ${template}`);
> 394	
> 395	  const frameDir = outDir ?? mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'hf-alpha-'));
> 396	  const browser = await chromium.launch({
> 397	    headless: true,
> 398	    // --allow-file-access-from-files: let the file:// template load its self-hosted @font-face (fonts/*.ttf).
> 399	    args: ['--no-sandbox', '--disable-dev-shm-usage', '--force-color-profile=srgb', '--disable-lcd-text', '--allow-file-access-from-files'],
> 400	  });
> 401	  try {
> 402	    const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width, height }, deviceScaleFactor: 1 });
> 403	    await page.goto(`file://${templatePath}`, { waitUntil: 'load' });
> 404	    await page.evaluate(
> 405	      (props) => (window as HfWindow).applyProps(props),
> 406	      { ...spec.props, fps, durationSec: durationS } as Record<string, unknown>,
> 407	    );
> 408	
> 409	    // Wait for the self-hosted @font-face to finish loading → no fallback-font early frames (deterministic).
> 410	    await page.evaluate(() => (document as { fonts?: { ready?: Promise<unknown> } }).fonts?.ready).catch(() => {});
> 411	
> 412	    for (let i = 0; i < totalFrames; i++) {
> 413	      await page.evaluate(([idx, total]) => (window as HfWindow).applyFrame(idx, total), [i, totalFrames] as [number, number]);
> 414	      await page.screenshot({
> 415	        path: join(frameDir, `f_${String(i).padStart(5, '0')}.png`),
> 416	        clip: { x: 0, y: 0, width, height },
> 417	        omitBackground: true,           // ← RGBA, transparent bg preserved (the overlay layer)
> 418	        animations: 'disabled',
> 419	      });
> 420	    }
> 421	    await browser.close();
> 422	
> 423	    const first = join(frameDir, 'f_00000.png');
> 424	    if (!existsSync(first) || statSync(first).size < 1024) throw new Error(`alpha_frames_too_small: ${first}`);
> 425	
> 426	    return { frameDir, framePattern: 'f_%05d.png', frames: totalFrames, fps, width, height };
> 427	  } finally {
> 428	    await browser.close().catch(() => {});
> 429	  }
> 430	}
> 431	
> 432	/**
> 433	 * Render a Studio-authored project — FR-VS-050 (SDD §VS-UI-C): NATIVE engine on @hyperframes/core (Apache-2.0)
> 434	 * by default; the legacy CLI wrap (FR-VS-047 §B.3) remains available as emergency rollback via
> 435	 * STUDIO_RENDER_ENGINE=cli. Throws on any failure (worker → finalize_video_render(failed, refund)).
> 436	 *
> 437	 * Security: `project_dir` MUST resolve strictly under STUDIO_PROJECTS_ROOT (no traversal / arbitrary path exec).
> 438	 * The guard runs HERE, engine-independent, before any FS/bundle/spawn work.
> 439	 */
> 440	export async function renderStudioProject(spec: StudioProjectSpec, outPath: string): Promise<RenderResult> {
> 441	  // Path-safety: dir must be the root or strictly inside it (blocks ../ traversal + absolute escapes).
> 442	  const dir = resolve(spec.project_dir ?? '');
> 443	  const root = resolve(STUDIO_PROJECTS_ROOT);
> 444	  if (dir !== root && !dir.startsWith(root + '/')) throw new Error(`project_dir_outside_root: ${dir}`);
> 445	  if (!existsSync(join(dir, 'index.html'))) throw new Error(`studio_project_not_found: ${join(dir, 'index.html')}`);
> 446	
> 447	  const engine = process.env.STUDIO_RENDER_ENGINE ?? 'native';
> 448	  return engine === 'cli'
> 449	    ? renderStudioProjectViaCli(dir, spec, outPath)
> 450	    : renderStudioProjectNative(dir, spec, outPath);
> 451	}
> 452	
> 453	/**
> 454	 * FR-VS-050 — native studio-project render. Algorithm (mapped empirically from the reference CLI,
> 455	 * Workflow wf_bbc8402d-096; SDD §VS-UI-C C.3):
> 456	 *   bundleToSingleHtml(dir) [runtime inline] → temp bundle INSIDE dir (file:// keeps relative refs working)
> 457	 *   → init-script window.__HF_EXPORT_RENDER_SEEK_CONFIG={fps} (runtime reads at init; fps is renderer-owned)
> 458	 *   → goto + wait (__renderReady && __player.getDuration()>0) + fonts
> 459	 *   → per frame: __player.renderSeek(i/fps) (synchronous deterministic seek, quantized floor(t*fps+1e-9)/fps
> 460	 *     runtime-side) → PNG screenshot → shared bitexact FFmpeg encode (NFR-VS-016).
> 461	 * Declared limits (§C.3): no virtual-time shim (adapter-driven compositions only — GSAP/css/waapi/anime/lottie/
> 462	 * three); embedded <video> not pre-extracted; external pinned CDN refs need network.
> 463	 */
> 464	async function renderStudioProjectNative(dir: string, spec: StudioProjectSpec, outPath: string): Promise<RenderResult> {
> 465	  const t0 = Date.now();
> 466	  const fps = Math.max(1, Math.min(60, spec.fps ?? 30));
> 467	  const resolution = spec.resolution && VALID_RESOLUTIONS.has(spec.resolution) ? spec.resolution : 'portrait';
> 468	  const { width, height } = RESOLUTION_DIMENSIONS[resolution];
> 469	
> 470	  // Apache-2.0 import-only usage (© HeyGen — LICENSE ships in node_modules/@hyperframes/core/LICENSE).
> 471	  const { bundleToSingleHtml } = await import('@hyperframes/core/compiler');
> 472	  const html: string = await bundleToSingleHtml(dir);
> 473	
> 474	  // Serve via file:// from INSIDE the project dir so relative asset refs keep resolving.
> 475	  const bundlePath = join(dir, `.hf-native-bundle-${process.pid}-${Date.now().toString(36)}.html`);
> 476	  writeFileSync(bundlePath, html, 'utf8');
> 477	
> 478	  const frameDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'hf-native-frames-'));
> 479	  const browser = await chromium.launch({
> 480	    headless: true,
> 481	    // Same determinism flags as the template path (single-tenant Usuário Zero, OTD-VA-011).
> 482	    args: ['--no-sandbox', '--disable-dev-shm-usage', '--force-color-profile=srgb', '--disable-lcd-text'],
> 483	  });
> 484	  try {
> 485	    const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width, height }, deviceScaleFactor: 1 });
> 486	    // fps is renderer-owned; the embedded runtime reads this at init (fallback 30). Must precede goto.
> 487	    await page.addInitScript((v) => { (window as HfWindow).__HF_EXPORT_RENDER_SEEK_CONFIG = { fps: v, fpsSource: 'mcorch-native-render' }; }, fps);
> 488	    await page.goto(`file://${bundlePath}`, { waitUntil: 'load', timeout: 60_000 });
> 489	
> 490	    // Readiness (mirrors the reference pollHfReady): timelines bound + deterministic adapters ready + duration real.
> 491	    await page.waitForFunction(
> 492	      () => (window as HfWindow).__renderReady === true
> 493	        && !!(window as HfWindow).__player
> 494	        && typeof (window as HfWindow).__player!.renderSeek === 'function'
> 495	        && (window as HfWindow).__player!.getDuration() > 0,
> 496	      undefined,
> 497	      { timeout: 45_000 },
> 498	    ).catch(() => { throw new Error('studio_runtime_not_ready: __renderReady/__player.getDuration()>0 not reached in 45s'); });
> 499	    await page.evaluate(() => (document as HfDocument).fonts?.ready);
> 500	
> 501	    const duration: number = await page.evaluate(() => (window as HfWindow).__player!.getDuration());
> 502	    if (!(duration > 0)) throw new Error('studio_composition_duration_zero'); // would produce a black video
> 503	    const totalFrames = Math.ceil(duration * fps);
> 504	
> 505	    for (let i = 0; i < totalFrames; i++) {
> 506	      // Synchronous deterministic seek: pauses timelines, seeks GSAP totalTime, fans out to adapters.
> 507	      await page.evaluate((t) => (window as HfWindow).__player!.renderSeek(t), i / fps);
> 508	      await page.screenshot({
> 509	        path: join(frameDir, `f_${String(i).padStart(5, '0')}.png`),
> 510	        clip: { x: 0, y: 0, width, height },
> 511	        animations: 'allow', // css adapter seeks CSS animations; 'disabled' would force them to final state
> 512	      });
> 513	    }
> 514	    await browser.close();
> 515	
> 516	    await encodePngFramesBitexact(frameDir, fps, outPath);
> 517	
> 518	    if (!existsSync(outPath)) throw new Error('studio_render_produced_no_file');
> 519	    const bytes = statSync(outPath).size;
> 520	    if (bytes < 100 * 1024) throw new Error(`studio_render_too_small: ${bytes} bytes (< 100KB, Lei 1 gate)`);
> 521	
> 522	    const probe = await ffprobeVideo(outPath);
> 523	    return { outPath, bytes, width: probe.width, height: probe.height, frames: probe.frames, fps: probe.fps, durationMs: Date.now() - t0 };
> 524	  } finally {
> 525	    await browser.close().catch(() => {});
> 526	    rmSync(frameDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
> 527	    try { unlinkSync(bundlePath); } catch { /* already gone */ }
> 528	  }
> 529	}
> 530	
> 531	/**
> 532	 * Legacy FR-VS-047 CLI wrap — emergency rollback only (STUDIO_RENDER_ENGINE=cli). The npm `hyperframes` artifact
> 533	 * ships no license grant (OTD-VS-020) — delete this path after the first editor-initiated native render passes.
> 534	 * ARM64 trap: the hyperframes-bundled Chrome does not run on ARM64 → point it at Playwright's chromium.
> 535	 */
> 536	async function renderStudioProjectViaCli(dir: string, spec: StudioProjectSpec, outPath: string): Promise<RenderResult> {
> 537	  const t0 = Date.now();
> 538	  const fps = Math.max(1, Math.min(60, spec.fps ?? 30));
> 539	  const resolution = spec.resolution && VALID_RESOLUTIONS.has(spec.resolution) ? spec.resolution : 'portrait';
> 540	  if (!existsSync(HF_CLI)) throw new Error(`hyperframes_cli_not_found: ${HF_CLI}`);
> 541	
> 542	  const browserPath = chromium.executablePath();
> 543	
> 544	  await new Promise<void>((res, rej) => {
> 545	    const p = spawn(HF_NODE, [
> 546	      HF_CLI, 'render', dir,
> 547	      '--resolution', resolution, '--fps', String(fps),
> 548	      '-o', outPath, '--quiet',
> 549	    ], {
> 550	      stdio: ['ignore', 'ignore', 'pipe'],
> 551	      env: { ...process.env, HYPERFRAMES_BROWSER_PATH: browserPath, HOME: process.env.HOME ?? '/home/ubuntu' },
> 552	    });
> 553	    let err = '';
> 554	    p.stderr.on('data', (d) => { err += d.toString(); });
> 555	    p.on('error', rej);
> 556	    p.on('close', (code) => (code === 0 ? res() : rej(new Error(`hyperframes_render_exit_${code}: ${err.slice(-800)}`))));
> 557	  });
> 558	
> 559	  if (!existsSync(outPath)) throw new Error('studio_render_produced_no_file');
> 560	  const bytes = statSync(outPath).size;
> 561	  if (bytes < 100 * 1024) throw new Error(`studio_render_too_small: ${bytes} bytes (< 100KB, Lei 1 gate)`);
> 562	
> 563	  const probe = await ffprobeVideo(outPath);
> 564	  return { outPath, bytes, width: probe.width, height: probe.height, frames: probe.frames, fps: probe.fps, durationMs: Date.now() - t0 };
> 565	}
> 566	
> 567	// ── CLI (material proof harness) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 568	if (import.meta.main) {
> 569	  const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
> 570	  const get = (k: string, d?: string) => {
> 571	    const i = argv.indexOf(`--${k}`);
> 572	    return i >= 0 && argv[i + 1] ? argv[i + 1] : d;
> 573	  };
> 574	  const out = get('out', '/tmp/hyperframes-test.mp4')!;
> 575	
> 576	  // Free image-to-video: --kenburns img.png [--kbdur 6] [--zoom in|out] (deterministic pan/zoom, US$0)
> 577	  const kbArg = get('kenburns');
> 578	  // Free "frames → video" mode: --images a.png,b.png,c.png [--sec 2.5] (Sovereign vision — HyperFrames joins pre-made frames)
> 579	  const imagesArg = get('images');
> 580	  const runner = kbArg
> 581	    ? encodeKenBurns(
> 582	        {
> 583	          image: kbArg,
> 584	          duration_s: Number(get('kbdur', '6')),
> 585	          zoom: (get('zoom', 'in') as 'in' | 'out'),
> 586	          fps: Number(get('fps', '30')),
> 587	        },
> 588	        out,
> 589	      )
> 590	    : imagesArg
> 591	    ? encodeImageSequence(
> 592	        {
> 593	          images: imagesArg.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean),
> 594	          seconds_per_image: Number(get('sec', '2.5')),
> 595	          fps: Number(get('fps', '30')),
> 596	        },
> 597	        out,
> 598	      )
> 599	    : renderComposition(
> 600	        {
> 601	          template: get('template', 'viral-short-9x16'),
> 602	          duration_s: Number(get('duration', '4')),
> 603	          fps: Number(get('fps', '30')),
> 604	          props: {
> 605	            hook: get('hook', 'Sua casa limpa sozinha'),
> 606	            caption: get('caption', 'O robô aspirador que faz tudo enquanto você vive a vida.'),
> 607	            cta: get('cta', 'Ver no Mercado Livre'),
> 608	            brand: get('brand', 'MCORCH'),
> 609	            product_image_url: get('image', ''),
> 610	          },
> 611	        },
> 612	        out,
> 613	      );
> 614	  runner
> 615	    .then((r) => {
> 616	      console.log(JSON.stringify(r, null, 2));
> 617	      console.log(`✅ rendered ${r.frames} frames → ${out} (${(r.bytes / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB, ${r.durationMs}ms)`);
> 618	    })
> 619	    .catch((e) => {
> 620	      console.error(`❌ render failed: ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : e}`);
> 621	      process.exit(1);
> 622	    });
> 623	}
> 624	
> ```

## 🤖 Claude *(20:48:14)*

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

I'm mapping out the complete architecture and identifying the vendored assets I need—the MediaPipe vision bundle, WASM files, and related dependencies that will power the face detection system. The real tension is whether to reference node_modules directly (fragile, not versioned) or vendor everything for robustness. The task explicitly asks for a clearly named, versioned directory for assets, so I should vendor the `.tflite` model into `scripts/video-repurpose/face-assets/` — and for consistency and self-containment, probably the bundle and wasm too, even though the design says only the model is "new." So the design actually intends for the WASM and bundle to come from node_modules (already vendorized by npm), with only the tflite model (230KB) as a new file to commit—keeping the git footprint minimal. Now I need to figure out where the runtime HTML page lives and how it imports these assets: either generate it dynamically in a temp directory with absolute node_modules paths, or commit a static HTML file.

The cleanest approach given this constraint is to generate the detector HTML at runtime in a temp directory, using absolute file:// imports to resolve the mediapipe bundle from node_modules and the vendored tflite model. This mirrors how render-core already generates frames in temp directories, and ESM imports can handle absolute file:// paths directly. replication is to keep the HTML and assets together in the vendor directory, but that creates a git footprint problem if I'm committing the bundle and wasm files. The real question is whether absolute file:// paths work for ESM imports—I should test that approach before deciding, since it might let me keep the HTML committed while resolving the vision library at runtime. The FilesetResolver needs a base path and fetches the wasm files relative to it — with file:// URLs and absolute paths, those fetch calls become `file:///abs/wasm/vision_wasm_internal.js` which should work with the right flags. The tricky part is that the bundle itself might do relative imports or compute paths dynamically, so I need to verify the actual import chain works end-to-end with absolute file URLs.

Let me de-risk this by testing it directly — I'll check if @mediapipe/tasks-vision is already a dependency in package.json and understand what the tsconfig validates, then write a quick test that points to the node_modules bundle and wasm files to see if the whole chain resolves correctly.

</details>

>  *
>  * Proves the picker lands the motion-graphics card in the VOID: it scores each candidate zone by edge-density
>  * agitation (edgedetect→signalstats YAVG) and chooses the calmest. Every fixture is synthesized with
>  * `ffmpeg lavfi` in a scratch dir — a flat gray half (calm) hstacked with a `testsrc` half (busy/detailed) —
>  * so the correct answer is known a priori. NEVER touches a real master, the network, or the DB. No mcoCoins.
>  *
>  * Gates:
>  *   G1 — subject (detail) at RIGHT over flat LEFT  → chosen zone on the LEFT (calm side).
>  *   G2 — subject (detail) at LEFT  over flat RIGHT → chosen zone on the RIGHT (calm side).
>  *   G3 — fail-open: a non-existent source → returns the deterministic `lower-left` default, `degraded:true`,
>  *        and does NOT throw (zone choice is cosmetic and must never break a render).
>  *   G4 — cost per cut is measured and reported (< ~2s), from the real `elapsedMs` of the G1/G2 picks (Lei 1).
>  *
>  * Usage: bun run scripts/qa/smoke-zone-saliency.ts
>  */
> import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
> import { join } from 'node:path';
> import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
> import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
> import { pickCalmestZone, DEFAULT_ZONE, type ZoneRect, type ZonePick } from '../video-repurpose/zone-saliency.ts';
> 
> let pass = true;
> function check(label: string, ok: boolean, detail = ''): void {
>   console.log(`${ok ? '✅' : '❌'} ${label}${detail ? `  → ${detail}` : ''}`);
>   if (!ok) pass = false;
> }
> 
> /** Horizontal center of a zone's placement box, in canvas fraction (0..1). < 0.5 = left, > 0.5 = right. */
> function centerX(rect: ZoneRect): number {
>   return rect.x + rect.w / 2;
> }
> 
> /**
>  * 3s 1080×1920 fixture: `leftBusy=false` → flat gray LEFT (calm) + testsrc RIGHT (busy); `leftBusy=true` swaps.
>  * testsrc animates, so the picker's 3-instant temporal MAX path is genuinely exercised.
>  */
> function makeSplitFixture(dir: string, name: string, leftBusy: boolean): string {
>   const path = join(dir, `${name}.mp4`);
>   const flat = 'color=c=gray:s=540x1920:r=30';
>   const busy = 'testsrc=s=540x1920:r=30';
>   const [left, right] = leftBusy ? [busy, flat] : [flat, busy];
>   const r = spawnSync('ffmpeg', [
>     '-y', '-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'error',
>     '-f', 'lavfi', '-i', left,
>     '-f', 'lavfi', '-i', right,
>     '-filter_complex', '[0:v][1:v]hstack=inputs=2,format=yuv420p',
>     '-t', '3', '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'ultrafast', '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p', path,
>   ]);
>   if (r.status !== 0) throw new Error(`fixture gen failed: ${r.stderr?.toString().slice(-300)}`);
>   return path;
> }
> 
> const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'smoke-zone-saliency-'));
> try {
>   const subjRight = makeSplitFixture(dir, 'subj-right', false); // detail on the RIGHT → calm LEFT
>   const subjLeft = makeSplitFixture(dir, 'subj-left', true);    // detail on the LEFT  → calm RIGHT
> 
>   // G1 — detail RIGHT ⇒ pick LEFT.
>   const p1: ZonePick = await pickCalmestZone(subjRight, 0, 3);
>   const g1ok = !p1.degraded && centerX(p1.rect) < 0.5 && p1.scores.length === 6;
>   check('G1 sujeito à DIREITA → zona escolhida à ESQUERDA (calma)', g1ok,
>     `zone=${p1.zone} centerX=${centerX(p1.rect).toFixed(3)} agitação=${p1.scores[0].agitation.toFixed(3)} degraded=${p1.degraded}`);
> 
>   // G2 — detail LEFT ⇒ pick RIGHT.
>   const p2: ZonePick = await pickCalmestZone(subjLeft, 0, 3);
>   const g2ok = !p2.degraded && centerX(p2.rect) > 0.5 && p2.scores.length === 6;
>   check('G2 sujeito à ESQUERDA → zona escolhida à DIREITA (calma)', g2ok,
>     `zone=${p2.zone} centerX=${centerX(p2.rect).toFixed(3)} agitação=${p2.scores[0].agitation.toFixed(3)} degraded=${p2.degraded}`);
> 
>   // G3 — fail-open on a missing source: deterministic default, no throw.
>   let g3ok = false, g3detail = '';
>   try {
>     const p3 = await pickCalmestZone(join(dir, 'does-not-exist.mp4'), 0, 3);
>     g3ok = p3.degraded === true && p3.zone === DEFAULT_ZONE;
>     g3detail = `zone=${p3.zone} degraded=${p3.degraded} (default=${DEFAULT_ZONE})`;
>   } catch (e) {
>     g3detail = `LANÇOU (viola fail-open): ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`;
>   }
>   check('G3 fonte inexistente → default lower-left, degraded, sem lançar', g3ok, g3detail);
> 
>   // G4 — measured cost per cut (the picker reports its own wall time). Budget ceiling ~2s.
>   const worst = Math.max(p1.elapsedMs, p2.elapsedMs);
>   const g4ok = p1.elapsedMs < 2000 && p2.elapsedMs < 2000;
>   check('G4 custo por corte medido (< 2s)', g4ok,
>     `G1=${p1.elapsedMs.toFixed(0)}ms G2=${p2.elapsedMs.toFixed(0)}ms pior=${worst.toFixed(0)}ms`);
> 
>   console.log(`\n${pass ? '✅ SMOKE PASS' : '❌ SMOKE FAIL'} — zone-saliency (escolha da zona mais calma para motion graphics)`);
> } catch (e) {
>   console.error('smoke error:', e instanceof Error ? e.message : e);
>   pass = false;
> } finally {
>   rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
> }
> process.exit(pass ? 0 : 1);
> ```

  "content": "<!doctype html>\n<html>\n<head><meta charset=\"utf-8\"></head>\n<body>\n<!--\n  Offline MediaPipe FaceDetector probe page (video-repurpose Fatia 2, OTD-VR-016).\n\n  Loaded via file:// by scripts/video-repurpose/face-detect.ts inside the SAME headless Chromium the render\n  engine uses (render-core.ts). EVERYTHING it needs is co-located and loaded with RELATIVE file:// paths \u2014\n  the ESM bundle, the WASM runtime dir, and the .tflite model \u2014 so NO network request is ever made at\n  runtime. The smoke (scripts/qa/smoke-face-aware-zone.ts) proves this by aborting every non-file:// request\n  and asserting blockedNetworkRequests === [] (US$0, air-gapped).\n\n  The detector is created ONCE (lazy) and cached on this page for the whole process, at a LOW confidence\n  floor; face-detect.ts filters detections by the caller's threshold in TS. That amortises the ~150-340ms\n  model+WASM init across every frame/clip of a worker run.\n-->\n<script type=\"module\">\n  import { FilesetResolver, FaceDetector } from './vision_bundle.mjs';\n\n  // Low creation-time floor; the TS caller re-filters by its own minConfidence. Creating the detector once\n  // at a fixed low floor lets us cache it and tune recall without rebuilding the graph.\n  const CREATE_CONFIDENCE_FLOOR = 0.2;\n\n  let _detectorPromise = null;\n  function getDetector() {\n    // Memoise the CREATION promise (not just the instance) so concurrent first calls share one init.\n    if (_detectorPromise) return _detectorPromise;\n    _detectorPromise = (async () => {\n      // forVisionTasks receives the LOCAL wasm dir (relative file://). The loader reads\n      // vision_wasm_internal.{js,wasm} from here \u2014 all file://, no CDN.\n      const fileset = await FilesetResolver.forVisionTasks('./wasm');\n      return FaceDetector.createFromOptions(fileset, {\n        baseOptions: {\n          modelAssetPath: './blaze_face_short_range.tflite', // local vendored model (file://)\n          delegate: 'CPU',\n        },\n        runningMode: 'IMAGE',\n        minDetectionConfidence: CREATE_CONFIDENCE_FLOOR,\n      });\n    })();\n    return _detectorPromise;\n  }\n\n  // Detect faces in one frame. `imageUrl` is a file:// URL (or data: URI) the caller decodes into an <img>.\n  // Returns bboxes in the frame's OWN pixel space (origin top-left); face-detect.ts normalises by workW/workH.\n  window.__runFaceDetect = async (imageUrl) => {\n    const detector = await getDetector();\n    const img = new Image();\n    await new Promise((res, rej) => {\n      img.onload = res;\n      img.onerror = () => rej(new Error('image_decode_failed'));\n      img.src = imageUrl;\n    });\n    const result = detector.detect(img);\n    return {\n      imageW: img.naturalWidth,\n      imageH: img.naturalHeight,\n      detections: (result.detections || []).map((d) => ({\n        x: d.boundingBox.originX,\n        y: d.boundingBox.originY,\n        w: d.boundingBox.width,\n        h: d.boundingBox.height,\n        score: d.categories && d.categories[0] ? d.categories[0].score : 0,\n      })),\n    };\n  };\n\n  window.__ready = true;\n</script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"
}

It is **not**: `package.json`\ndeclares `@mediapipe/camera_utils`, `drawing_utils`, and `hands`, but **not** `tasks-vision`. It is only\ntransitively present. A plain `npm ci` / `bun install` would therefore **not** restore it, and the detector\nwould silently fail-open to Fatia 1 forever \u2014 an invisible regression. Vendoring the small bundle (~140 KB) +\nthe SIMD WASM (~9.7 MB) makes the feature **version-independent and genuinely self-contained** (Law 1 \u2014\nrobustness over an undeclared transitive dependency). The ~10 MB one-time git cost buys an air-gapped,\nself-hosted vision runtime, in line with the ecosystem doctrine \"self-host\u00e1vel, custo USD externo = 0\".\n\n**SIMD only:** headless Chromium (the exact Playwright build the render pipeline pins) supports WASM SIMD, so\nonly the SIMD variant is shipped. If a future host ever lacks SIMD, `FilesetResolver` fails to load the\nruntime \u2192 `detectFaceRegions` **fails open** (returns `[]`, `degraded:true`) \u2192 placement degrades to the\nFatia 1 calm-zone behavior. Never a crash.\n\n## Refreshing the runtime (design-time only)\n\n```bash\nNM=node_modules/@mediapipe/tasks-vision\ncp $NM/vision_bundle.mjs                 scripts/video-repurpose/face-assets/vision_bundle.mjs\ncp $NM/wasm/vision_wasm_internal.js      scripts/video-repurpose/face-assets/wasm/\ncp $NM/wasm/vision_wasm_internal.wasm    scripts/video-repurpose/face-assets/wasm/\n# The model is pinned; re-download blaze_face_short_range.tflite from the MediaPipe model card only to bump it.\nxxd -s4 -l4 scripts/video-repurpose/face-assets/blaze_face_short_range.tflite   # must print 'TFL3'\nbun run scripts/qa/smoke-face-aware-zone.ts   # re-prove offline + detection + exclusion before committing\n```\n\n## License / attribution\n\nMediaPipe Tasks (`@mediapipe/tasks-vision`) and the BlazeFace model are \u00a9 Google, Apache-2.0. For the forbidden case, scores would only contain survivors, which is arguably cleaner semantically since excluded zones aren't real candidates anyway. The existing assertions should be fine since they only run with no forbidden zones.

The question is whether to include excluded zones in the scores array for telemetry and debugging visibility, but to keep the contract simple and avoid breaking the "scores[0] === chosen" guarantee, I'll stick with just the survivors. documentation comment. The excluded zones don't need to be tracked in the scores array for observability since segment-core already logs which zones were excluded and the smoke tests verify the chosen zone's overlap — that's sufficient. So I'll stick with scores containing only the candidate zones.

Now let me trace through the smoke test scenarios: on the baseline case with no forbidden zones, a calm-right fixture should pick the right side. When I add the forbidden constraint excluding the right half, all right zones get filtered out, leaving only the left zones as survivors. Among those left zones, the algorithm picks the calmest one. But on a busy-left fixture where the left zones have high agitation and the right zones are calm but excluded, the survivors are just the left zones, so it has to pick from those busy zones.

For the overlap grid sampling, I'm implementing a function that takes a zone rectangle and an array of forbidden rectangles, then samples a grid of points within the zone to count how many fall inside any forbidden area, returning the ratio.

Writing overlap detection... Setting up the constants for face exclusion and overlap tolerance, then updating the ZonePick interface to track whether faces were excluded. I'm also adding the forbidden rectangles to the saliency options and exporting the type. In segment-core, I need to detect face regions at key frames before picking the calmest zone, passing those forbidden areas through the pipeline.

The challenge is synchronizing the sample times—zone-saliency computes start/mid/end internally in sampleTimes, but I need those exact same instants in segment-core to call detectFaceRegions. Rather than duplicating the logic, the cleanest approach is to export sampleTimes from zone-saliency so both places use the same calculation.

Now I'm verifying the timing semantics: sampleTimes takes absolute source seconds (inSec/outSec), and since pickCalmestZone receives the clip's absolute boundaries and detectFaceRegions extracts frames using absolute ffmpeg timestamps, passing the computed times directly should align everything correctly.

For the motion graphics style branch, I'm setting the zone to default and then detecting faces at those same sampled instants to ensure the card placement avoids landing on faces even when the calmest zone overlaps with the subject. The detector is fail-open, so any errors just pass through without throwing. Writing face detection logic... Adding imports to segment-core for the zone-saliency and face-detection utilities, then checking that playwright is already in the dependency graph through render-core, so there's no conflict. I need to import the `NormRect` type from face-detect to type the forbidden zones array, and I'm verifying the function signature matches what I'm expecting.

The smoke tests should still pass since `pickCalmestZone` handles the new `forbidden` option gracefully when it's not provided, and the new `faceExcludedAll` field won't break existing destructuring. The `sampleTimes` function signature is straightforward, so I'll export it as-is and use it in segment-core.

Now I'm ready to write face-detect.ts, keeping performance considerations in mind.

Detection is a refinement, not a guarantee.\n *\n * Anticipated-process SSOT (Lei 2): docs/processes/motion-graphics-face-aware-placement.md.\n */\n\nimport { spawn } from 'node:child_process';\nimport { existsSync, mkdtempSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { join, dirname } from 'node:path';\nimport { tmpdir } from 'node:os';\nimport { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';\nimport { chromium, type Browser, type Page } from 'playwright';\n\nconst __dirname = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Vendored, offline detector runtime (see face-assets/README.md) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nconst FACE_ASSETS_DIR = join(__dirname, 'face-assets');\nconst DETECTOR_HTML = join(FACE_ASSETS_DIR, 'detector.html');\nconst BUNDLE_MJS = join(FACE_ASSETS_DIR, 'vision_bundle.mjs');\nconst MODEL_TFLITE = join(FACE_ASSETS_DIR, 'blaze_face_short_range.tflite');\nconst WASM_BINARY = join(FACE_ASSETS_DIR, 'wasm', 'vision_wasm_internal.wasm');\n\nconst DEFAULT_WORK_W = 608;   // 9:16 analysis-frame width (proven detection size, design-time)\nconst DEFAULT_WORK_H = 1080;  // 9:16 analysis-frame height\nconst DEFAULT_MIN_CONFIDENCE = 0.4; // recall-biased: better to over-avoid than place text on a missed face\nconst MAX_SAMPLES = 6;        // cap sampled instants to bound cost (segment-core passes 1..3)\n\n/** A rectangle in canvas fractions (0..1) of the reframed 9:16 frame \u2014 the exact space as ZONE_RECTS. */\nexport interface NormRect {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  w: number;\n  h: number;\n}\n\nexport interface FaceDetectOptions {\n  /** Reframe aspect of the analysis frame (master is center-cropped to this). Default 9/16. */\n  aspect?: number;\n  /** Analysis frame width; the frame is reframed+scaled to this before detection. Default 608. */\n  workW?: number;\n  /** Analysis frame height. Default 1080. Keep the 9:16 aspect. */\n  workH?: number;\n  /** Minimum detection score to treat a box as a real face. Default 0.4. Clamped 0..1. */\n  minConfidence?: number;\n  /** FFmpeg binary. Empty = no face found. */\n  forbidden: NormRect[];\n  /** Bounding box of `forbidden` (min-x/min-y \u2192 max), or null when empty. A missing asset \u2192 fail-open (no face regions). */\nfunction assetsPresent(): boolean {\n  return existsSync(DETECTOR_HTML) && existsSync(BUNDLE_MJS) && existsSync(MODEL_TFLITE) && existsSync(WASM_BINARY);\n}\n\n/**\n * Lazily launch (or reuse) the cached detector page. Args MIRROR render-core's alpha path \u2014 notably\n * `--allow-file-access-from-files`, so the file:// page can import the vendored bundle + load the WASM/model.\n * Returns null on any launch/load failure (fail-open \u2014 the caller then reports degraded).\n */\nasync function ensureDetectorPage(): Promise<Page | null> {\n  if (_page && _browser && _browser.isConnected()) return _page;\n  try {\n    if (!_browser || !_browser.isConnected()) {\n      _browser = await chromium.launch({\n        headless: true,\n        args: [\n          '--no-sandbox',\n          '--disable-dev-shm-usage',\n          '--force-color-profile=srgb',\n          '--disable-lcd-text',\n          '--allow-file-access-from-files',\n        ],\n      });\n      registerCleanup();\n    }\n    const page = await _browser.newPage();\n    await page.goto(pathToFileURL(DETECTOR_HTML).href, { waitUntil: 'load' });\n    await page.waitForFunction('window.__ready === true', undefined, { timeout: 30_000 });\n    _page = page;\n    return _page;\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.warn(`[repurpose] face-detect browser init failed (fail-open, no face regions): ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`);\n    try { await _browser?.close(); } catch { /* ignore */ }\n    _browser = null;\n    _page = null;\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n/** Graceful teardown for the host worker (systemd stop). Idempotent; safe to call when nothing was launched. */\nexport async function closeFaceDetector(): Promise<void> {\n  try { await _browser?.close(); } catch { /* ignore */ }\n  _browser = null;\n  _page = null;\n}\n\nfunction clamp(v: number, lo: number, hi: number): number {\n  return v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v;\n}\n\n/** Sanitise caller instants: finite, \u22650, dedupe near-identical (<0.02s), preserve order, cap at MAX_SAMPLES. */\nfunction sanitizeTimes(times: number[]): number[] {\n  const out: number[] = [];\n  for (const t of Array.isArray(times) ? times : []) {\n    if (!Number.isFinite(t)) continue;\n    const v = Math.max(0, t);\n    if (out.some((u) => Math.abs(u - v) < 0.02)) continue;\n    out.push(v);\n    if (out.length >= MAX_SAMPLES) break;\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** Source-agnostic center-crop to `aspect`, then scale to (w,h). MIRRORS segment-core/zone-saliency reframe so\n * the detected bbox lands in exactly the pixels the render will overlay. Commas inside min() escaped for FFmpeg. */\nfunction reframeChain(aspect: number, w: number, h: number): string {\n  const cw = `min(iw\\\\,ih*${aspect})`;\n  const ch = `min(ih\\\\,iw/${aspect})`;\n  return `crop=${cw}:${ch}:(iw-ow)/2:(ih-oh)/2,scale=${w}:${h}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop=${w}:${h},setsar=1`;\n}\n\n/** Extract ONE reframed frame at instant `t` to `outPath` (PNG). Resolves false on any FFmpeg failure. */\nfunction extractReframedFrame(\n  ffmpegPath: string, sourcePath: string, t: number, w: number, h: number, aspect: number, outPath: string,\n): Promise<boolean> {\n  const args = [\n    '-v', 'error', '-y',\n    '-ss', Math.max(0, t).toFixed(3),\n    '-i', sourcePath,\n    '-frames:v', '1',\n    '-vf', reframeChain(aspect, w, h),\n    outPath,\n  ];\n  return new Promise((resolvePromise) => {\n    let p;\n    try {\n      p = spawn(ffmpegPath, args, { stdio: ['ignore', 'ignore', 'pipe'] });\n    } catch {\n      resolvePromise(false);\n      return;\n    }\n    p.on('error', () => resolvePromise(false));\n    p.on('close', (code: number | null) => resolvePromise(code === 0));\n  });\n}\n\n/** Clamp a fractional rect into [0,1] and reject degenerate boxes. Returns null when the box is unusable. */\nfunction normalizeClamp(x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number): NormRect | null {\n  if (![x, y, w, h].every(Number.isFinite)) return null;\n  const nx = clamp(x, 0, 1);\n  const ny = clamp(y, 0, 1);\n  const nw = clamp(w, 0, 1 - nx);\n  const nh = clamp(h, 0, 1 - ny);\n  if (nw <= 0 || nh <= 0) return null;\n  return { x: nx, y: ny, w: nw, h: nh };\n}\n\n/** Bounding box of a set of rects (min-x/min-y \u2192 max-x2/max-y2), or null when empty. */\nfunction unionRect(rects: NormRect[]): NormRect | null {\n  if (!rects.length) return null;\n  let minX = 1, minY = 1, maxX = 0, maxY = 0;\n  for (const r of rects) {\n    minX = Math.min(minX, r.x);\n    minY = Math.min(minY, r.y);\n    maxX = Math.max(maxX, r.x + r.w);\n    maxY = Math.max(maxY, r.y + r.h);\n  }\n  return { x: minX, y: minY, w: Math.max(0, maxX - minX), h: Math.max(0, maxY - minY) };\n}\n\n/**\n * Detect face(s) over `times` and return their union as a FORBIDDEN region for text placement.\n *\n * @param sourcePath  local path to the master (the FOOTAGE \u2014 detection runs on the footage, not the render).\n * @param times       absolute source seconds to sample (typically the SAME start/mid/end zone-saliency uses).\n * @param opts        aspect/workW/workH/minConfidence/ffmpegPath overrides.\n *\n * Fail-open contract: NEVER throws. Any failure \u2192 `{ forbidden: [], merged: null, degraded: true }`.\n */\nexport async function detectFaceRegions(\n  sourcePath: string,\n  times: number[],\n  opts: FaceDetectOptions = {},\n): Promise<FaceRegionResult> {\n  const t0 = performance.now();\n  const workW = Math.max(2, Math.floor(opts.workW ?? DEFAULT_WORK_W));\n  const workH = Math.max(2, Math.floor(opts.workH ?? DEFAULT_WORK_H));\n  const aspect = opts.aspect && opts.aspect > 0 ? opts.aspect : 9 / 16;\n  const minConfidence = clamp(opts.minConfidence ?? DEFAULT_MIN_CONFIDENCE, 0, 1);\n  const ffmpegPath = opts.ffmpegPath ?? 'ffmpeg';\n\n  const degrade = (): FaceRegionResult =>\n    ({ forbidden: [], merged: null, degraded: true, sampled: 0, elapsedMs: performance.now() - t0 });\n\n  try {\n    if (!existsSync(sourcePath)) return degrade();\n    if (!assetsPresent()) {\n      console.warn(`[repurpose] face-detect assets missing under ${FACE_ASSETS_DIR} \u2014 fail-open (no face regions)`);\n      return degrade();\n    }\n    const sampleTimes = sanitizeTimes(times);\n    if (sampleTimes.length === 0) {\n      // Nothing to sample \u2014 not a failure, just no face knowledge \u2192 behaves as Fatia 1 (empty forbidden).\n      return { forbidden: [], merged: null, degraded: false, sampled: 0, elapsedMs: performance.now() - t0 };\n    }\n    const page = await ensureDetectorPage();\n    if (!page) return degrade();\n\n    const forbidden: NormRect[] = [];\n    let sampled = 0;\n    const frameDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'face-detect-'));\n    try {\n      for (const t of sampleTimes) {\n        const framePath = join(frameDir, `f_${sampled}.png`);\n        const ok = await extractReframedFrame(ffmpegPath, sourcePath, t, workW, workH, aspect, framePath);\n        if (!ok || !existsSync(framePath)) continue; // fail-soft per instant; other instants may still detect\n        let out: PageDetectResult;\n        try {\n          out = await page.evaluate(\n            (u) => (window as FaceWindow).__runFaceDetect(u),\n            pathToFileURL(framePath).href,\n          ) as PageDetectResult;\n        } catch (e) {\n          console.warn(`[repurpose] face-detect eval failed at t=${t.toFixed(2)}s (skip): ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`);\n          continue;\n        }\n        sampled++;\n        const iw = out.imageW || workW;\n        const ih = out.imageH || workH;\n        for (const d of out.detections || []) {\n          if ((d.score ?? 0) < minConfidence) continue;\n          const r = normalizeClamp(d.x / iw, d.y / ih, d.w / iw, d.h / ih);\n          if (r) forbidden.push(r);\n        }\n      }\n    } finally {\n      rmSync(frameDir, { recursive: true, force: true });\n    }\n\n    // Not one frame made it through the detector \u2192 we genuinely could not measure \u2192 degraded (fall back to Fatia 1).\n    if (sampled === 0) return degrade();\n\n    return {\n      forbidden,\n      merged: unionRect(forbidden),\n      degraded: false,\n      sampled,\n      elapsedMs: performance.now() - t0,\n    };\n  } catch (e) {\n    // Contract is no-throw; reaching here is unexpected. Fail open, never surface to the render pipeline.\n    console.warn(`[repurpose] face-detect threw (fail-open, no face regions): ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`);\n    return degrade();\n  }\n}\n"

}

  "new_string": "/** YAVG band width under which two zones count as \"equally calm\" (edge-map luma units). Buckets the score so\n * near-identical calm regions defer to PREFERENCE instead of chasing sub-perceptual float noise. */\nconst CALM_EPS = 0.5;\n\n/** Fatia 2: a zone whose fraction covered by the face union exceeds this is EXCLUDED from selection. 0.12 \u2248\n * \"more than a corner of the card would clip the face\" \u2014 validated tolerant enough to keep a viable zone in the\n * common single-face-off-center case, strict enough to reject a zone the face materially intrudes on. */\nconst EXCLUDE_FRAC = 0.12;\n\n/** Fatia 2: overlap band for the all-excluded fallback tie-break (bucket face-overlap so the least-covered zone\n * wins by a meaningful margin, then defer to calmness/preference). */\nconst OVERLAP_EPS = 0.02;\n\n/** Fatia 2: grid resolution for zone\u00d7face overlap sampling. 24\u00d724 = 576 point-in-rect tests per zone \u2014 exact\n * enough for the union of a handful of face rects, deterministic, and negligible cost (pure arithmetic). */\nconst OVERLAP_GRID = 24;"

{

  "old_string": "/** A zone is selectable only if its footprint clears the card minimum. */\nfunction zoneFits(zone: Zone): boolean {\n  const r = ZONE_RECTS[zone];\n  return r.w >= MIN_ZONE_W_FRAC && r.h >= MIN_ZONE_H_FRAC;\n}",
  "new_string": "/** A zone is selectable only if its footprint clears the card minimum. */\nfunction zoneFits(zone: Zone): boolean {\n  const r = ZONE_RECTS[zone];\n  return r.w >= MIN_ZONE_W_FRAC && r.h >= MIN_ZONE_H_FRAC;\n}\n\n/** A forbidden rect is usable only if finite and non-degenerate. */\nfunction isUsableForbidden(r: ForbiddenRect): boolean {\n  return [r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h].every(Number.isFinite) && r.w > 0 && r.h > 0;\n}\n\n/** Point-in-rect (half-open) in canvas fractions. */\nfunction pointInRect(px: number, py: number, r: ForbiddenRect): boolean {\n  return px >= r.x && px < r.x + r.w && py >= r.y && py < r.y + r.h;\n}\n\n/**\n * Fraction of a zone's area covered by the UNION of the forbidden rects (Fatia 2). Grid-samples the zone and\n * counts points falling inside ANY forbidden rect \u2014 so overlapping face rects are unioned correctly (no\n * double-counting), for any arrangement, deterministically. Returns 0 when there is nothing forbidden.\n */\nfunction zoneFaceOverlap(zone: ZoneRect, forbidden: readonly ForbiddenRect[]): number {\n  if (!forbidden.length) return 0;\n  const N = OVERLAP_GRID;\n  let inside = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {\n    const px = zone.x + ((i + 0.5) / N) * zone.w;\n    for (let j = 0; j < N; j++) {\n      const py = zone.y + ((j + 0.5) / N) * zone.h;\n      if (forbidden.some((r) => pointInRect(px, py, r))) inside++;\n    }\n  }\n  return inside / (N * N);\n}"
}
```
